However, recent studies have proven that adversarial attacks can significantly degrade the inference ability of VLMs, posing substantial risks to their practical applications. Prevalent test-time adaptation methods typically rely on multi-view augmentation to implement various fine-tuning strategies, which struggle to identify semantic information and are prone to destroying discriminative regions in fine-grained scenarios. To address these limitations, we propose Attention-Guided Test-Time Prompt Tuning (A-TPT), a semantics-preserving method designed for test-time adaptation. We first refine the gradient attention rollout mechanism to identify semantically meaningful regions surviving under adversarial attacks. Furthermore, we leverage them to guide the spatially varying augmentation intensities and multi-view ensemble for prompt tuning and inference. Extensive experiments demonstrate that A-TPT outperforms existing test-time adaptation methods on both adversarial and clean data. Codes are available at https://github.com/SEU-VIPGroup/A-TPT .

</details>


### [73] [World-Ego Modeling for Long-Horizon Evolution in Hybrid Embodied Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19957)
*Zuyao Lin,Jianhui Zhang,Peidong Jia,Xiaoguang Zhao,Shanghang Zhang,Xingyu Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出世界-自我建模（World-Ego Modeling）新范式，将未来演化解耦为世界（指令无关的场景规律）和自我（以机器人为中心的指令条件动态）两个组件。基于运动、语义和意图三个视角定义边界，提出后解耦、前解耦和全解耦三种策略。实例化为世界-自我模型（WEM），包含隐式分离的世界-自我规划器和级联并行混合专家（CP-MoE）扩散生成器。同时构建了首个混合导航-操作长程世界建模基准HTEWorld（125K视频片段、4.5M+帧、2K+指令）。实验表明WEM在HTEWorld上达到SOTA，在纯操作基准上保持竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型将世界演化和自我演化耦合在一个流中，导致在长程具身场景（尤其是混合导航与操作的交织任务）中性能退化。作者旨在通过解耦世界和自我组件来解决这一根本性局限。

Method: 提出世界-自我建模范式，从运动、语义和意图三个视角定义解耦边界，设计后解耦、前解耦和全解耦三种策略。实例化WEM：采用隐式分离的世界-自我规划器进行未来预测，配合级联并行混合专家（CP-MoE）扩散生成器生成视频预测。

Result: 在HTEWorld基准上达到SOTA性能，同时在现有纯操作基准上保持竞争力。HTEWorld包含125K视频片段（4.5M+帧）和300条多轮评估轨迹（2K+指令）。

Conclusion: 世界-自我解耦范式有效解决了长程混合任务中世界模型性能退化问题，为具身智能中的世界模型研究提供了新的概念框架和实现路径。

Abstract: World models are widely explored in embodied intelligence, yet they typically predict distinct evolutions of the world and the ego within a single stream, where the world captures persistent instruction-agnostic scene regularities and the ego captures robot-centric instruction-conditioned dynamics. This world-ego entanglement leads to a degradation in long-horizon embodied scenarios, particularly in hybrid tasks with interleaved navigation and manipulation behaviors. In this paper, we introduce \emph{World-Ego Modeling}, a new conceptual paradigm that decomposes future evolution into world and ego components. We define the world-ego boundary from three perspectives, i.e., motion-, semantic-, and intention-based views, and analyze three disentanglement strategies with post-, pre-, and full disentanglement. Further, we instantiate this paradigm as the World-Ego Model (WEM), a unified embodied world model that couples an implicit separate world-ego planner with a cascade-parallel mixture-of-experts (CP-MoE) diffusion generator. To enable rigorous evaluation, we further construct HTEWorld, the first benchmark for long-horizon world modeling with hybrid navigation-manipulation tasks, providing 125K video clips (over 4.5M frames) with fine-grained action annotations and 300 multi-turn evaluation trajectories (over 2K instructions). Extensive experiments show that WEM achieves state-of-the-art performance on HTEWorld while remaining competitive on existing manipulation-only benchmarks.

</details>


### [74] [SphericalDreamer: Generating Navigable Immersive 3D Worlds with Panorama Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19974)
*Antoine Schnepf,Karim Kassab,Flavian Vasile,Andrew Comport*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SphericalDreamer提出了一种从文本提示生成完全沉浸式、可长距离导航的360°户外3D环境的方法，通过生成多个全景图像并提升为3D，同时保持视觉和几何一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法无法同时满足两个关键需求：长距离空间导航和全覆盖360°×180°视场角，限制了沉浸式3D环境的生成能力。

Method: 基于多个全景图像的生成，随后将其提升至3D空间并融合，同时确保视觉与几何一致性。

Result: 生成的3D环境高度详细、完全沉浸，相比先前方法在规模和可导航性上有显著提升。

Conclusion: SphericalDreamer有效解决了现有方法在长距离可导航性与全向视场覆盖上的局限性，为沉浸式3D环境生成提供了新途径。

Abstract: The generation of immersive and navigable 3D environments is increasingly prevalent with the growing adoption of virtual reality and 3D content. However, recent methods face a fundamental limitation: they cannot produce 3D worlds that simultaneously (i) are navigable over long-range spatial extents and (ii) cover the complete omnidirectional field of view ($360^\circ$ horizontally and $180^\circ$ vertically). To address this challenge, we introduce SphericalDreamer, a method for generating fully immersive and long-range 3D outdoor environments from textual prompts. Our approach is built on the generation of multiple panoramic images, which are subsequently lifted into 3D and fused together while maintaining visual and geometric consistency. SphericalDreamer produces highly detailed, fully immersive 3D environments, while substantially improving scale and navigability compared to prior approaches.

</details>


### [75] [RECIPE: Procedural Planning via Grounding in Instructional Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19976)
*Luigi Seminara,Antonino Furnari,Lorenzo Torresani*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出RECIPE方法，利用视频中ASR文本的接地质量作为GRPO的奖励信号，将海量噪声教学视频转化为验证器而非标签源，从而提升视觉规划任务性能。在7个基准上，RECIPE-RL相比基线提升7-16个点，超越监督微调，并保持生成多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉规划任务受限于标注瓶颈：现有数据集规模小、领域窄且编码单条执行轨迹；大规模教学视频虽丰富但ASR伪标签存在分割和对齐错误。关键不对称性在于：从噪声视频中提取干净步骤标签困难，但验证步骤序列是否在ASR文本中有时间接地却成本低廉且可扩展。

Method: 提出RECIPE框架，利用接地质量作为GRPO的奖励信号，将噪声教学语料库转化为验证器。统一适用于两种规划器输入配置（Socratic和Video），以及标注和弱监督两种场景。

Result: 在7个程序性基准上，RECIPE-RL在0.5B、3B、7B三个规模下均超越基线：域内宏平均准确率提升7-8个点，零样本场景提升高达16个点。优于监督微调（伪标签SFT会降低基模型性能）。在VPA基准上超越最强零样本基线，在COIN上保持SFT所破坏的生成多样性。

Conclusion: 利用"验证比提取容易"这一不对称性，通过RL将噪声视频语料转化为可扩展的验证信号，是提升视觉规划的有效范式，无需人工标注即可实现显著且鲁棒的改进。

Abstract: Visual planning asks a model to generate the remaining steps of a procedure in natural language given a partial video context and a goal. Progress on this task is bottlenecked by annotation: clean labeled datasets are small, domain-narrow, and encode a single execution trajectory per example, even though many valid orderings exist. Large-scale instructional video corpora offer orders of magnitude more procedural content, but supervised fine-tuning on pseudo-labels from their noisy ASR narrations propagates segmentation and alignment errors and stays single-trajectory. We identify a key asymmetry: extracting clean step labels from noisy video is hard, but verifying whether a generated step sequence is temporally grounded in ASR transcripts is cheap and scales to millions of videos via precomputed text embeddings. We exploit this asymmetry in RECIPE, which uses grounding quality as a reward for GRPO, turning the noisy corpus into a verifier rather than a label source. The framework applies uniformly to two planner input configurations (Socratic, with a textual history extracted by a frozen VLM, and Video, consuming video tokens directly) and to annotated and weakly supervised regimes. We evaluate on 7 procedural benchmarks using a reference-based LLM-as-judge protocol scoring plans across 6 procedural criteria. RECIPE-RL improves over the base checkpoint at all scales (0.5B, 3B, 7B) and every benchmark, with macro-accuracy gains of +7 to +8 points in-domain and up to +16 points zero-shot. It outperforms supervised fine-tuning on both annotated and pseudo-labeled plans (the latter degrades the base) and remains robust without human annotations. Used as the proposal stage of a prior propose-assess-search planner, it improves over the strongest zero-shot baseline at every horizon on Visual Planning for Assistance, and on COIN it preserves the generation diversity that SFT collapses.

</details>


### [76] [InterLight: Leveraging Intrinsic Illumination Priors for Low-Light Image Enhancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19982)
*Ziqi Wang,Xu Zhang,Laibin Chang,Shi Chen,Jiaqi Ma,Huan Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出InterLight框架，通过系统挖掘和利用内在光照先验来解决低光照图像增强问题，采用物理引导增强、自适应提示、亮度门控记忆机制和自监督一致性约束，实现对光照的解耦与鲁棒增强，在多个基准上取得更优效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于Retinex理论的深度学习方法在低光照图像增强中存在过增强、颜色失真、均匀噪声或理想光照假设等局限性，缺乏对内在光照先验的系统性利用。

Method: InterLight包含四个核心组件：(1) 传感器级光照响应先验的物理引导数据增强；(2) 基于场景潜在光照状态的自适应提示退化表征；(3) 亮度门控内在记忆机制，选择性补偿暗区信息损失并保持亮区保真度；(4) 自监督一致性目标正则化，蒸馏光照不变特征。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上的大量实验表明，该方法能产生更清晰的纹理和更视觉一致的增强结果，优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 通过深度挖掘内在光照先验，InterLight有效解决了过增强和颜色失真问题，为LLIE提供了更鲁棒的光照感知增强框架。

Abstract: Low-Light Image Enhancement (LLIE) has long been a challenging problem in low-level vision, as insufficient illumination often leads to low contrast, detail loss, and noise. Recent studies show that deep learning-based Retinex theory can effectively decouple illumination and reflectance. However, existing methods frequently suffer from over-enhancement or color distortion, and often assume uniform noise or ideal lighting. To address these limitations, we propose InterLight, a novel framework that systematically excavates and operationalizes intrinsic illumination priors for LLIE.Our core insight is that robust enhancement requires not just estimating illumination, but constructing an illumination-aware pipeline. We first inject sensor-level illumination-response priors via physics-guided augmentation, then represent the degradation through adaptive prompts conditioned on the scene's latent illumination state. This explicit representation directly guides a luminance-gated intrinsic memory mechanism to selectively compensate for information loss, prioritizing reconstruction in dark regions while preserving fidelity in bright ones. Finally, the entire process is regularized by a self-supervised consistency objective that distills illumination-invariant features. By deeply exploiting intrinsic illumination priors, our method achieves clearer textures and more visually coherent enhancement results. Extensive experiments across multiple benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach. Code is available at: https://github.com/House-yuyu/InterLight.

</details>


### [77] [CogOmniControl: Reasoning-Driven Controllable Video Generation via Creative Intent Cognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19995)
*Hongji Yang,Songlian Li,Yucheng Zhou,Xiaotong Zhao,Alan Zhao,Chengzhong Xu,Jianbing Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CogOmniControl是一个推理驱动的可控视频生成框架，通过专门的CogVLM模型从稀疏抽象条件中认知用户创意意图，并结合CogOmniDiT扩散模型实现多条件统一控制，形成闭环架构，在专业视频生成任务上超越现有开源模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型在视频生成中面对抽象、稀疏或复杂条件时表现脆弱，无法在专业生产流程（如故事板草图、黏土渲染条件）中满足用户创意意图。现有方法通过适配器注入条件或耦合通用视觉语言模型，存在能力差距，无法产生与用户创意意图对齐的视频。

Method: 1) 训练专门化的CogVLM模型，使用真实动漫生产数据，能够从稀疏抽象条件中准确认知用户创意意图并生成密集推理输出；2) CogOmniDiT通过上下文生成统一多种条件控制，并通过强化学习与CogVLM推理输出对齐；3) 利用CogVLM的鲁棒能力规划评估器，实现Best-of-N视频选择，形成闭环"harness-like"架构；4) 构建CogReasonBench和CogControlBench专业基准。

Result: 在两个专业基准（CogReasonBench和CogControlBench）上的实验表明，CogOmniControl超越了现有开源模型。

Conclusion: 将推理能力引入可控视频生成，通过专门化的VLM从抽象条件中认知创意意图，结合扩散模型生成与强化学习对齐，实现了更专业、更符合用户意图的视频生成，并构建了闭环评估选择架构。

Abstract: Recent diffusion models achieve strong photorealism and fluency in video generation, yet remain fragile under abstract, sparse or complex conditions, leading to poor performance in professional production workflows such as storyboard sketches and clay render conditions. Existing video generation models, either inject conditions through adapters or couple a generic vision-language model (VLM) within a diffusion backbone, leaving a capability gap and failing to produce the videos that align with the user's creative intent. We present CogOmniControl, a reasoning-driven framework that factorizes controllable video generation into creative intent cognition and generation. Specifically, we train a specialized CogVLM using authentic anime production data. Compared to generic VLMs, it generates more professional and clear outputs, accurately cognizing user creative intent from sparse and abstract conditions and tuning these cues into dense reasoning output. Besides, CogOmniDiT unifies the controls from various conditions through in-context generation and is aligned to the CogVLM reasoning outputs via reinforcement learning. Furthermore, leveraging CogVLM's robust capability in guiding video generation, we release its potential in planning specific evaluators and enable a Best-of-N selection for the generated videos. This integration transforms the entire framework into a closed-loop "harness-like" architecture. We further introduce CogReasonBench and CogControlBench, built from professional workflows data that carry genuine creative intent rather than simulated ones. Experiments on two benchmarks show that CogOmniControl surpassed the existing open-source models. The project website: https://um-lab.github.io/CogOmniControl/

</details>


### [78] [A Nash Equilibrium Framework For Training-Free Multimodal Step Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20033)
*Rohit Sinha,Kunal Tilaganji,Tanuja Ganu,Nagarajan Natarajan,Amit Sharma,Vineeth N. Balasubramanian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种无需训练的验证方法，将逐步推理验证建模为专业评判者之间的纳什均衡博弈，利用评判者之间的共识与分歧信号来检测多模态大语言模型推理链中的错误，在六个基准上取得2.4%-5.2%的持续提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型的推理链常含有细微错误，现有验证方法存在局限：学习型评判器需要大量标注数据且跨任务表现不一致；现有无训练方法仅简单平均不同来源的分数，忽略了分数分歧本身蕴含的推理步骤有效性信息。

Method: 将逐步验证建模为多个专业评判者之间的协调问题，形式化为纳什均衡博弈：评判者一致表示步骤有效，分歧揭示不稳定。通过闭式解计算均衡分数，实现分歧感知过滤和稳定性感知排序。

Result: 在六个基准上相比基线模型提升2.4%至5.2%，与学习型评判器竞争性能相当，证明跨模态一致信号（而非仅平均置信度）能提供稳健的验证信号，且无需任务特定适配。

Conclusion: 跨模态一致性能作为无需训练的鲁棒验证信号，克服了现有方法忽视分歧信息价值的缺陷。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models often generate reasoning chains containing subtle errors that lead to incorrect answers. Current verification approaches have notable limitations. Learned critics need extensive labeled data and show inconsistent performance across different tasks. Meanwhile, existing training-free methods simply average scores from different sources, missing a key insight: when these scores disagree, that disagreement itself carries important information about whether a reasoning step is truly valid or not. We propose a training-free verification approach that treats step-wise verification as a coordination problem among specialized judges. We formalize these judges' interaction as a Nash equilibrium game where agreement signals valid steps while disagreement reveals instability. Our method computes equilibrium scores through a closed-form solution, enabling both disagreement-aware filtering and stability-conscious ranking of reasoning steps. Evaluated across six benchmarks, our approach achieves consistent improvements of 2.4% to 5.2% over baseline models and shows competitive performance against learned critics, demonstrating that cross-modal agreement (not just average confidence) provides robust verification signals without task-specific adaptation.

</details>


### [79] [Stage-adaptive Token Selection for Efficient Omni-modal LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20035)
*Zijie Xin,Jie Yang,Ruixiang Zhao,Tianyi Wang,Fengyun Rao,Jing Lyu,Xirong Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SEATS是一种针对全模态大语言模型的无训练、阶段自适应token选择方法，通过逐层分析跨模态token重要性，在保留仅10%视觉和音频token的情况下实现9.3倍FLOPs减少和4.8倍预填充加速，同时保持96.3%的原始性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有全模态大语言模型处理密集非文本token导致巨大计算开销，而现有的无训练token选择方法要么只关注纯视觉输入，要么在LLM之前以固定模态比例剪枝，未能捕捉跨模态token重要性在层间的演化规律。

Method: SEATS包含三个阶段的剪枝策略：在LLM之前通过注意力加权多样性选择去除时空冗余；在LLM内部按块逐步剪枝token，并利用查询相关性分数动态分配模态间保留预算；在深层完全移除所有剩余非文本token。

Result: 在Qwen2.5-Omni和Qwen3-Omni上的实验表明，仅保留10%的视觉和音频token，SEATS实现了9.3倍FLOPs减少和4.8倍预填充加速，同时保持96.3%的原始性能。

Conclusion: 本文通过揭示全模态LLM中视觉和音频token依赖性的逐层演化规律，证明了跨模态融合后深层非文本token的高度冗余性，并据此设计出阶段自适应的无训练剪枝方法，有效平衡了推理效率与性能。

Abstract: Omni-modal large language models (om-LLMs) achieve unified audio-visual understanding by encoding video and audio into temporally aligned token sequences interleaved at the window level. However, processing these dense non-textual tokens throughout the LLM incurs substantial computational overhead. Although training-free token selection can reduce this cost, existing methods either focus on visual-only inputs or prune om-LLM tokens only before the LLM with fixed per-modality ratios, failing to capture how cross-modal token importance evolves across layers. To address this limitation, we first analyze the layer-wise token dependency of om-LLMs. We find that visual and audio dependencies follow a block-wise pattern and gradually weaken with depth, indicating that many late-layer non-textual tokens become redundant after cross-modal fusion. Motivated by this observation, we propose SEATS, a training-free, stage-adaptive token selection method for efficient om-LLM inference. Before the LLM, SEATS removes spatiotemporal redundancy via attention-weighted diversity selection. Inside the LLM, it progressively prunes tokens across blocks and dynamically allocates the retention budget from temporal windows to modalities using query relevance scores. In late layers, it removes all remaining non-textual tokens once cross-modal fusion is complete. Experiments on Qwen2.5-Omni and Qwen3-Omni demonstrate that SEATS effectively improves inference efficiency. Retaining only 10% of visual and audio tokens, it achieves a 9.3x FLOPs reduction and a 4.8x prefill speedup while preserving 96.3% of the original performance.

</details>


### [80] [OP2GS: Object-Aware 3D Gaussian Splatting with Dual-Opacity Primitives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20044)
*Guiyu Liu,Niklas Vaara,Janne Mustaniemi,Juho Kannala,Janne Heikkilä*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OP2GS通过为每个3D高斯原语附加显式实例身份和专用实例不透明度σ*，将对象感知能力引入3D高斯泼溅。该双不透明度公式解耦了视觉存在与实例占用，使错误标记的高斯体在保持图像渲染能力的同时，在对象掩码分支中透明化。通过随机对象损失优化实例占用场，并在对象级别附加语义描述符，显著降低了计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3DGS方法在场景理解任务中面临核心挑战：基于特征的方案需要为每个高斯存储高维嵌入，导致巨大的存储和推理开销；而基于2D掩码提升的管道则遭受标签污染问题——在2D到3D投影过程中，对视觉重建必要的高斯体常被分配错误的对象标签，且缺乏有效的处理机制。

Method: 提出双不透明度机制：原始不透明度σ负责视觉重建，新增实例不透明度σ*控制高斯体对特定对象掩码的贡献。引入随机对象损失，利用3DGS的标准透射率可见性来优化1D实例占用场。语义描述符通过多视图聚合在对象级别附加，而非逐高斯存储。

Result: 在开放词汇场景理解任务中，OP2GS达到了与特征训练方法竞争的性能，同时显著降低了计算开销。相比无训练管道，通过物理一致的占用学习解决了可见性歧义问题。

Conclusion: OP2GS通过解耦视觉重建与实例占用的双不透明度设计，有效解决了标签污染问题，在保持高效渲染的同时实现了对象级场景理解，为高斯泼溅在开放词汇理解任务中提供了轻量级且鲁棒的解决方案。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) provides an explicit and efficient scene representation, but its primitives lack inherent object-level identity, hindering downstream tasks such as open-vocabulary scene understanding. Existing methods typically address this by either distilling high-dimensional feature embeddings into Gaussians or by lifting 2D mask labels into 3D via heuristic refinement. However, feature-based approaches incur heavy storage and decoding overhead, while lifting-based pipelines remain vulnerable to label contamination: Gaussians necessary for appearance reconstruction often receive incorrect object labels during 2D-to-3D projection. We propose OP2GS, an object-aware Gaussian representation that augments each primitive with an explicit instance identity and a dedicated instance opacity $σ^{*}$ for object-mask rendering. The original opacity $σ$ remains responsible for visual reconstruction, while $σ^{*}$ models whether a Gaussian should contribute to a particular object mask. This dual-opacity formulation decouples visual existence from instance occupancy: mislabeled Gaussians can remain available for image rendering while becoming transparent in the object-mask branch. To learn this representation, we introduce a random object loss that optimizes the 1D instance occupancy field using the standard transmittance-based visibility of 3DGS. Semantic descriptors are then attached at the object level through multi-view aggregation, eliminating per-Gaussian feature storage. Compared with feature-training approaches, OP2GS achieves competitive open-vocabulary performance while significantly reducing computational overhead. Compared with training-free pipelines, it leverages physically consistent occupancy learning to resolve visibility ambiguities.

</details>


### [81] [X-Ray cardiac angiographic vessel segmentation based on pixel classification using machine learning and region growing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20073)
*E O Rodrigues,L O Rodrigues,J J Lima,D Casanova,F Favarim,E R Dosciatti,V Pegorini,L S N Oliveira,F F C Morais*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于像素分类的X射线血管造影图像血管分割方法，结合各向异性扩散、Hessian矩阵、数学形态学和统计特征，采用ELEMENT方法论（像素分类与区域生长相互影响），使用随机森林分类器，以95.48%的准确率超越现有无监督方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: X射线血管造影图像中血管的精确分割对临床诊断至关重要，但现有无监督方法性能有限，亟需更准确的血管分割方法。

Method: 方法包括：(1)从每个像素邻域提取多类纹理特征（各向异性扩散、Hessian矩阵特征、数学形态学特征和统计特征）；(2)采用ELEMENT方法论，即像素分类结果受区域生长控制，同时分类结果又影响后续像素的分类；(3)使用随机森林分类器预测像素是否属于血管结构。

Result: 该方法在X射线血管造影图像分割任务中取得了文献中最佳准确率95.48%，优于当前最先进的无监督方法。

Conclusion: 所提出的有监督像素分类方法结合多模态纹理特征和ELEMENT迭代分类机制，在血管分割任务上显著优于无监督方法，验证了该方法的有效性和优越性。

Abstract: This work proposes a pixel-classification approach for vessel segmentation in x-ray angiograms. The proposal uses textural features such as anisotropic diffusion, features based on the Hessian matrix, mathematical morphology and statistics. These features are extracted from the neighborhood of each pixel. The approach also uses the ELEMENT methodology, which consists of creating a pixel-classification controlled by region-growing where the result of the classification affects further classifications of pixels. The Random Forests classifier is used to predict whether the pixel belongs to the vessel structure. The approach achieved the best accuracy in the literature (95.48%) outperforming unsupervised state-of-the-art approaches.

</details>


### [82] [Probability-Conserving Flow Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20079)
*Parsa Esmati,Junha Hyung,Amirhossein Dadashzadeh,Jaegul Choo,Majid Mirmehdi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出AdaMaG方法，通过连续性方程分析引导机制，将引导效应分解为散度项和分数平行项，证明散度项在接近数据流形时结构性地爆炸，并据此设计自适应时间表来约束这两项，实现了无额外推理成本的高质量引导生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CFG等引导方法采用启发式的线性组合，忽略了生成流形的几何结构，导致概率不守恒，强引导下样本偏离学习到的流形。

Method: 通过连续性方程分析引导机制，将引导效应分解为散度项和分数平行项；证明散度项在接近数据流形时存在结构性爆炸；据此设计时间相关的时间表配合分数平行衰减，提出即插即用的AdaMaG规则，同时对两项进行约束。

Result: 在图像生成基准上，AdaMaG提升了真实感、减少了幻觉、并在高引导区域实现了受控的去饱和效果。

Conclusion: 大多数关于减少饱和或提升生成质量的启发式方法可直接对应到分解中的两项；AdaMaG在不增加推理成本的前提下有效改善了生成质量。

Abstract: Diffusion and flow-based generative models dominate visual synthesis, with guidance aligning samples to user input and improving perceptual quality. However, Classifier-Free Guidance (CFG) and extrapolation-based methods are heuristic linear combinations of velocities/scores that ignore the generative manifold geometry, breaking probability conservation and driving samples off the learned manifold under strong guidance. We analyse guidance through the continuity equation and show its effect decomposes into a divergence term and a score-parallel term defined invariantly across parameterisations. We prove the divergence term blows up structurally as sampling approaches the data manifold, motivating a time-dependent schedule alongside score-parallel attenuation. The resulting plug-and-play rule, Adaptive Manifold Guidance (AdaMaG), bounds both terms at no additional inference cost. Finally, we show that most empirical heuristics for reducing saturation or improving generation quality correspond directly to the two terms in our decomposition. Across image generation benchmarks, AdaMaG improves realism, reduces hallucinations, and induces controlled desaturation in high-guidance regimes.

</details>


### [83] [Spatially Prompted Visual Trajectory Prediction for Egocentric Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20085)
*Yifan Li,Xinyu Zhou,Yunhao Ge,Yu Kong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文首次形式化定义了空间提示视觉轨迹预测（SP-VTP）任务，通过初始空间提示（如边界框或点）指定操作目标，预测未来末端执行器轨迹。为此构建了EgoSPT数据集，并提出SPOT模型，在场景级分割下显著优于非提示或单源提示基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人操作任务通常通过语言指令或任务标识符指定，但在物体相似的杂乱环境中，空间指示（指向要操作的物体及其放置位置）更为有效。本文旨在解决视觉中心的对象和目标指定难题，提出一种利用空间提示来定义任务目标的轨迹预测新范式。

Method: 提出SPOT模型，包含三个模块：任务编码器（处理第一帧视觉特征和坐标空间提示）、观测编码器（处理当前视觉和历史上下文）、轨迹生成器（预测未来末端执行器运动）。同时构建EgoSPT数据集，包含第一帧对象和目标标注以及恢复的3D末端执行器运动轨迹。

Result: 在严格的场景级分割下，SPOT在跨场景轨迹预测上显著优于非提示或单源提示基线方法。

Conclusion: EgoSPT和SPOT共同建立了SP-VTP这一新的空间提示问题，为自我中心操作提供了一种简单且可扩展的任务条件范式。

Abstract: Robotic manipulation is often specified through language instructions or task identifiers, yet cluttered environments with similar objects are better handled by spatially indicating what to move and where to place it. Addressing the vision-centric challenge of object and goal specification, we present, to the best of our knowledge, the first formalization of Spatially Prompted Visual Trajectory Prediction (SP-VTP). This novel setting utilizes initial spatial prompts (like bounding boxes or points) to define task objectives, tasking the model with forecasting future end-effector trajectories from egocentric streams. To study this problem, we collect and annotate EgoSPT, a dataset of egocentric spatially prompted manipulation trajectories with first-frame object and target grounding annotations and recovered 3D end-effector motion. SP-VTP is challenging because the task specification is static, while the scene configuration evolves over time. To solve this problem, we propose SPOT(Spatially Prompted Object-Target Policy), which combines a task encoder for first-frame visual and coordinate spatial prompts, an observation encoder for current visual and history context, and a trajectory generator for future end-effector motion. Experiments under strict scene-level splits show that SPOT improves cross-scene trajectory prediction over non-prompted or single-source prompted baselines. Together, EgoSPT and SPOT establish a new spatial prompting problem SP-VTP, as a simple and scalable task condition for egocentric manipulation.

</details>


### [84] [MetaEarth-MM: Unified Multimodal Remote Sensing Image Generation with Scene-centered Joint Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20090)
*Zhiping Yu,Chenyang Liu,Jinqi Cao,Qinzhe Yang,Siwei Yu,Zhengxia Zou,Zhenwei Shi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了MetaEarth-MM，一个用于多模态遥感图像的统一生成基础模型，采用场景中心联合建模范式，支持五类模态的配对联合生成和任意模态间转换，并构建了包含280万张全球图像的大规模数据集EarthMM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态遥感图像对地球观测至关重要，但实际中完整配对观测数据稀缺。现有生成方法通常通过孤立的逐对模态转换处理该问题，但随着模态数量和生成任务的增加，其通用性和可扩展性受限。

Method: 提出场景中心联合建模范式，采用解耦架构：首先从可用观测中推断潜在场景表征，然后以该中间状态为条件生成目标模态，替代了传统的基于外观层面的跨模态直接映射。

Result: 构建了包含280万张多分辨率全球图像及220万对齐配对的EarthMM数据集；实验表明MetaEarth-MM在多种生成任务上表现出强生成能力和鲁棒泛化性，并在数据和表征层面支持下游任务。

Conclusion: MetaEarth-MM作为一种通用基础模型，在多模态跨模态地球观测中展现出巨大潜力，能够统一完成多模态联合生成与任意模态间转换。

Abstract: Multi-modal remote sensing images are vital for Earth observation, yet complete paired observations are often scarce in practice. Existing generative methods commonly address this problem through isolated pairwise modality translation, but their versatility and scalability remain limited as the number of modalities and generation tasks increases. Here, we develop a generative foundation model MetaEarth-MM for multi-modal remote sensing imagery, enabling paired joint generation and any-to-any translation across five modalities within a unified model. Recognizing the intrinsic scene consistency underlying multi-modal observations, we introduce a scene-centered joint modeling paradigm in MetaEarth-MM. Unlike previous methods that rely on direct appearance-level cross-modal mapping, our model organizes the generation around the underlying scene content. Specifically, MetaEarth-MM adopts a decoupled architecture that first infers a latent scene representation from available observations, and then generates target modalities conditioned on this intermediate state. To support training, we further construct EarthMM, a large-scale dataset comprising 2.8 million multi-resolution global images with 2.2 million aligned pairs. Extensive experiments demonstrate that MetaEarth-MM not only exhibits strong generative capability and robust generalization across diverse generation tasks, but also supports downstream tasks at both data and representation levels, highlighting its potential as a general foundation model for cross-modal Earth observation. The code and dataset will be available at https://github.com/YZPioneer/MetaEarth-MM.

</details>


### [85] [SetCon: Towards Open-Ended Referring Segmentation via Set-Level Concept Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20110)
*Zhixiong Zhang,Yizhuo Li,Shuangrui Ding,Yuhang Zang,Shengyuan Ding,Long Xing,Yibin Wang,Qiaosheng Zhang,Jiaqi Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SetCon方法，将开放指代分割重新定义为显式集合级概念预测，利用LVLM生成的自然语言概念替代特殊token进行联合掩码解码，在图像和视频基准上均取得SOTA结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LVLM-based指代分割方法将多个目标视为独立输出而非连贯集合，缺乏对集合级属性（如完整性和互斥性）的捕捉能力，难以处理多实例、跨类别组或开放目标集的复杂场景。

Method: 1) 提出SetCon框架，将开放指代分割转化为显式集合级概念预测；2) 使用LVLM生成的自然语言概念作为语义条件进行联合掩码集解码；3) 层次化语义分解：先预测共享的集合级概念定义目标范围，再细化到与目标子集对齐的细粒度概念组；4) 两阶段标注流程，扩展现有推理分割数据集（236k样本，784k概念短语）。

Result: 图像基准：gRefCOCO上+3.3 gIoU，MUSE上+12.1 gIoU，且随着目标数量增加优势扩大；视频基准：7个基准上取得SOTA，包括MeViS上+10.9 J&F，Ref-SeCVOS上+12.4 J&F。

Conclusion: 用自然语言概念替代专用分割token作为集合级语义条件，能有效建模目标集合的完整性和互斥性，显著提升开放场景指代分割性能，且该概念接口可无缝迁移到视频任务。

Abstract: Referring segmentation grounds natural-language queries to pixel-level masks, but extending it to complex scenarios with multiple instances, cross-category groups, or open-ended target sets remains challenging. Previous Large Vision Language Model (LVLM)-based methods represent referred targets with one or more special tokens sequentially, treating multiple targets as separate outputs rather than a coherent set and offering little incentive to capture set-level properties such as completeness and mutual exclusivity. We reformulate open-ended referring segmentation as explicit set-level concept prediction and propose Set-Concept Segmentation (SetCon), which uses LVLM-generated natural-language concepts, instead of segmentation-specific tokens, as semantic conditions for joint mask-set decoding. A hierarchical semantic decomposition first predicts a shared set-level concept defining the target scope and then refines it into fine-grained concept groups aligned with target subsets. To support this, a two-stage annotation pipeline augments existing reasoning segmentation datasets with hierarchical semantic supervision (236k samples, 784k concept phrases). SetCon achieves state-of-the-art results on image benchmarks (+3.3 gIoU on gRefCOCO, +12.1 gIoU on MUSE), with margins that grow as the number of referred targets increases. The concept interface also transfers to video under a detect-and-track setting, yielding new state-of-the-art results on seven referring video benchmarks, including +10.9 J&F on MeViS and +12.4 J&F on Ref-SeCVOS.

</details>


### [86] [PixVerve: Advancing Native UHR Image Generation to 100MP with a Large-Scale High-Quality Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20147)
*Haojun Chen,Haoyang He,Chengming Xu,Qingdong He,Junwei Zhu,Yabiao Wang,Zhucun Xue,Xianfang Zeng,Zhennan Chen,Xiaobin Hu,Hao Zhao,Yong Liu,Jiangning Zhang,Dacheng Tao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了PixVerve-95K数据集（95K张超高清图像，每张≥100M像素）及配套评估基准PixVerve-Bench，通过三种训练策略将现有T2I模型扩展至原生100MP生成，并构建了涵盖视觉质量和语义对齐的综合评估体系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有T2I模型在高分辨率（1K-2K）上有进展，但对超高清（UHR）图像生成的需求日益增长，而高分辨率内容的稀缺性和复杂性构成了巨大挑战。目前缺乏高质量、开源的大规模UHR图像-文本数据集和相应的评估标准。

Method: 1) 构建PixVerve-95K数据集：设计了专门的数据管道，收集95K张最小像素数100M的多样化场景图像，附带七维标注；2) 提出三种训练方案将现有T2I基础模型扩展至原生100MP生成；3) 建立PixVerve-Bench基准，结合传统指标和多模态大语言模型评估，对UHR图像的视觉质量和语义对齐进行全面评估。

Result: 成功构建了PixVerve-95K高质量开源UHR数据集，并在其基础上通过三种训练策略实现了T2I模型向100MP原生生成的拓展。实验结果和训练策略的探索为后续研究提供了有价值的洞见。

Conclusion: 本文首次系统性地解决了超高清（100MP）文本到图像生成中的数据集构建、模型扩展和评估标准三大问题，为未来UHR图像生成研究奠定了坚实基础。

Abstract: Text-to-Image (T2I) models have recently seen notable progress around 1K and 2K resolution. With the extreme desire for better visual experience and the rapid development of imaging technology, the demand for Ultra-High-Resolution (UHR) image generation has grown significantly. However, UHR image generation poses great challenges due to the scarcity and complexity of high-resolution content. In this paper, we first introduce PixVerve-95K, a high-quality, open-source UHR T2I dataset curated with a carefully designed data pipeline, which contains 95K images across diverse scenarios (each image has a minimum pixel-count of 100M) and seven-dimensional annotations. Based on our large-scale image-text dataset, we take a pioneering step to extend various T2I foundation models to native 100MP generation with three training schemes. Finally, leveraging both conventional metrics and multimodal large language model-based assessments, our proposed PixVerve-Bench benchmark establishes a comprehensive evaluation protocol for UHR images encompassing visual quality and semantic alignment. Extensive experimental results on our benchmark and the constructive exploration of training strategies collaboratively provide valuable insights for future breakthroughs.

</details>


### [87] [TideGS: Scalable Training of Over One Billion 3D Gaussian Splatting Primitives via Out-of-Core Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20150)
*Chonghao Zhong,Linfeng Shi,Hua Chen,Tiecheng Sun,Hao Zhao,Binhang Yuan,Chaojian Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TideGS提出了一个基于SSD-CPU-GPU三级存储层次的外核训练框架，通过块虚拟化几何、层次化异步流水线和轨迹自适应差分流式传输三项技术，在单张24GB GPU上实现了超过十亿个3D高斯原语的训练，远超此前约1亿个的规模限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练大规模3D高斯泼溅时，每个高斯原语携带大属性向量，参数表超过GPU显存容量，此前系统受限于单GPU上数千万个高斯原语，亟需突破显存瓶颈以支持十亿级原语的训练。

Method: TideGS采用三项协同技术：1）块虚拟化几何，将参数按SSD对齐的空间局部性组织；2）层次化异步流水线，重叠I/O与计算；3）轨迹自适应差分流式传输，仅传输相邻迭代间工作集的增量变化。

Result: 在单张24GB GPU上可训练超过十亿个高斯原语，在大规模场景中达到单GPU基线中最佳重建质量，规模远超此前外核基线（约1亿个）和标准显存训练（约1100万个）。

Conclusion: TideGS通过将GPU显存视为工作集缓存而非持久参数存储，利用3DGS训练固有的稀疏性和轨迹条件特性，有效突破了显存容量对训练规模的限制。

Abstract: Training 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) at billion-primitive scale is fundamentally memory-bound: each Gaussian primitive carries a large attribute vector, and the aggregate parameter table quickly exceeds GPU capacity, limiting prior systems to tens of millions of Gaussians on commodity single-GPU hardware. We observe that 3DGS training is inherently sparse and trajectory-conditioned: each iteration activates only the Gaussians visible from the current camera batch, so GPU memory can serve as a working-set cache rather than a persistent parameter store. Building on this insight, we introduce TideGS, an out-of-core training framework that manages parameters across an SSD-CPU-GPU hierarchy via three synergistic techniques: block-virtualized geometry for SSD-aligned spatial locality, a hierarchical asynchronous pipeline to overlap I/O with computation, and trajectory-adaptive differential streaming that transfers only incremental working-set deltas between iterations. Experiments show that TideGS enables training with over one billion Gaussians on a single 24 GB GPU while achieving the best reconstruction quality among evaluated single-GPU baselines on large-scale scenes, scaling beyond prior out-of-core baselines (e.g., approximately 100M Gaussians) and standard in-memory training (e.g., approximately 11M Gaussians).

</details>


### [88] [Multi-axis Analysis of Image Manipulation Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20174)
*Keanu Nichols,Divya Appapogu,Giscard Biamby,Dina Bashkirova,Anna Rohrbach,Bryan A. Plummer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了AUDITS基准，包含超过53万张来自用户和新闻照片的图像，用于评估图像篡改检测方法在域偏移、质量、类型和大小等多个维度上的鲁棒性，实验采用基于扩散模型的修复技术生成多样化篡改，揭示了现有方法在域泛化方面的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然图像篡改软件日益先进且易于使用（特别是生成式AI的进步），篡改图像可能传播 misinformation、制造虚假叙事并影响公众对重要议题的看法，但目前针对跨不同视觉域的先进篡改检测研究十分有限。

Method: 构建AUDITS基准数据集（530K+图像，来自用户照片和新闻照片两个不同来源），使用基于扩散模型的修复技术生成多样化篡改，设计多轴分析框架（域偏移、质量、类型、大小），对现有图像篡改检测方法进行全面实验评估。

Result: 通过在不同类型的域偏移下进行实验，评估了现有图像篡改检测方法的鲁棒性，揭示了现有方法在跨域泛化方面的不足。

Conclusion: AUDITS基准为图像篡改检测领域提供了新的见解，有助于推动开发更可靠、更具泛化能力的检测方法。

Abstract: Advanced image editing software enables easy creation of highly convincing image manipulations, which has been made even more accessible in recent years due to advances in generative AI. Manipulated images, while often harmless, could spread misinformation, create false narratives, and influence people's opinions on important issues. Despite this growing threat, there is limited research on detecting advanced manipulations across different visual domains. Thus, we introduce Analysis Under Domain-shifts, qualIty, Type, and Size (AUDITS), a comprehensive benchmark designed for studying axes of analysis in image manipulation detection. AUDITS comprises over 530K images from two distinct sources (user and news photos). We curate our dataset to support analysis across multiple axes using recent diffusion-based inpaintings, spanning a diverse range of manipulation types and sizes. We conduct experiments under different types of domain shift to evaluate robustness of existing image manipulation detection methods. Our goal is to drive further research in this area by offering new insights that would help develop more reliable and generalizable image manipulation detection methods.

</details>


### [89] [MSAVBench: Towards Comprehensive and Reliable Evaluation of Multi-Shot Audio-Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20183)
*Yujie Wei,Yujin Han,Zhekai Chen,Yongming Li,Kaixun Jiang,Zhihang Liu,Quanhao Li,Zhiwu Qing,Xiang Wang,Zhen Xing,Ruihang Chu,Lingyi Hong,Yefei He,Junjie Zhou,Junqiu Yu,Yang Shi,Difan Zou,Kai Zhu,Shiwei Zhang,Yingya Zhang,Yu Liu,Xihui Liu,Hongming Shan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MSAVBench是首个面向多镜头音视频生成的综合基准与自适应混合评估框架，覆盖视频、音频、镜头、参考四个维度，支持最多15个镜头，通过自适应自校正分割、实例级评分和工具化证据提取等机制实现91.5%的斯皮尔曼等级相关，系统评估19个模型后发现当前系统在导演级控制和细粒度音视频同步上仍存在挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准在数据多样性和评估流水线方面存在局限性，缺乏对多镜头音视频生成模型的系统性、可靠评估方法。

Method: 构建涵盖视频、音频、镜头、参考四维度的基准数据集，提出自适应混合评估框架，包含自适应自校正镜头分割机制、实例级评分规则和工具化证据提取方法。

Result: 在19个闭源和开源模型上的系统评估表明，当前系统在导演级控制和细粒度音视频同步方面仍存在困难，模块化或智能体式生成流水线有望缩小开源与闭源模型之间的差距。

Conclusion: MSAVBench填补了多镜头音视频生成评估的空白，验证了所提评估框架与人类判断的高度一致性，为未来研究提供了基准数据和评估代码。

Abstract: Video generation is rapidly evolving from single-shot synthesis to complex multi-shot audio-video (MSAV) narratives to meet real-world demands. However, evaluating such frontier models remains a fundamental challenge. Existing benchmarks are limited in scope and data diversity, and rely on rigid evaluation pipelines, preventing systematic and reliable assessment of modern MSAV models. To bridge these gaps, we introduce MSAVBench, the first comprehensive benchmark and adaptive hybrid evaluation framework for multi-shot audio-video generation. Our benchmark spans four key dimensions, video, audio, shot, and reference, covering diverse task settings, varying shot counts of up to 15, and challenging non-realistic scenarios. Our evaluation framework improves robustness through an adaptive self-correction mechanism for shot segmentation, instance-wise rubrics for subjective metrics, and tool-grounded evidence extraction for complex judgments. Furthermore, MSAVBench achieves high alignment with human judgments, reaching a Spearman rank correlation of 91.5%. Our systematic evaluation of 19 state-of-the-art closed- and open-source models shows that current systems still struggle with director-level control and fine-grained audio-visual synchronization, while modular or agentic generation pipelines offer a promising path toward narrowing the gap between open- and closed-source models. We will release the benchmark data and evaluation code to facilitate future research.

</details>


<div id='cs.CL'></div>

# cs.CL [[Back]](#toc)

### [90] [The Annotation Scarcity Paradox in Low-Resource NLP Evaluation: A Decade of Acceleration and Emerging Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19066)
*Vukosi Marivate*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 低资源NLP评估面临严重的"标注稀缺悖论"：模型扩展能力远超人类主权评估基础设施，威胁到报告进展的认知有效性。论文通过批判性叙事调查（2014年至今），提出从交易式数据提取转向关系性、社区嵌入评估的范式转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低资源NLP领域虽取得爆发式增长，但其评估体系存在一个未被充分审视的深层张力：评估日益复杂的生成系统所需的社会语言学专业知识严重不足、分布不均且被结构性边缘化。论文旨在揭示这一矛盾并推动变革。

Method: 采用批判性叙事调查方法，梳理低资源NLP评估从2014年至今的三个发展阶段：早期启发式乐观主义、自上而下基准扩展的幻觉、以及当前生成式瓶颈期。通过分析抽取式数据管道、低补偿"幽灵工作"和语言数据燃尽等现象，提出"标注稀缺悖论"概念框架，并评述数据增强、基于模型的评估、参与式策展、项目反应理论和主动学习等新兴应对策略。

Result: 识别并概念化了"标注稀缺悖论"——技术模型扩展能力与人类主权评估基础设施之间的结构性摩擦。揭示了当前评估实践中的认知有效性危机，包括数据集中的偏见、评估劳动的剥削以及语言数据资源的不可持续消耗。

Conclusion: 克服评估瓶颈需要从交易式数据提取转向关系性、社区嵌入的评估范式，其基础应是认知治理、数据主权和共享所有权。这是对从业者的行动呼吁。

Abstract: Over the past decade, low-resource natural language processing (NLP) has experienced explosive growth, propelled by cross-lingual transfer, massively multilingual models, and the rapid proliferation of benchmarks. Yet this apparent progress masks a critical, insufficiently examined tension: the deep sociolinguistic expertise required to evaluate increasingly complex generative systems is severely strained, inequitably distributed, and structurally marginalised. We present a critical narrative survey of low-resource NLP evaluation (2014--present), tracing its evolution across three phases: early heuristic optimism, the illusions of top-down benchmark scaling, and the current era of generative bottlenecks. We conceptualise the \emph{Annotation Scarcity Paradox}, the structural friction arising when the technical capacity to scale models vastly outpaces the sovereign human infrastructure required to authentically evaluate them. By examining extractive data pipelines, undercompensated ``ghost work'', and language data flaring, we argue that this paradox threatens the epistemic validity of reported progress. We survey emerging responses -- including data augmentation, model-based evaluation, participatory curation, and annotation-efficient approaches via item response theory and active learning -- and assess their equity and validity trade-offs. We close with a practitioner call to action, arguing that overcoming this bottleneck requires a paradigm shift from transactional data extraction to relational, community-embedded evaluation rooted in epistemic governance, data sovereignty, and shared ownership.

</details>


### [91] [Benchmarking Commercial ASR Systems on Code-Switching Speech: Arabic, Persian, and German](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19069)
*Sajjad Abdoli,Ghassan Al-Sumaidaee,Clayton W. Taylor,Ahmad,ElShiekh,Ahmed Rashad*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个面向代码-switching（语码转换）场景的ASR基准测试，覆盖四种语言对（埃及阿拉伯语-英语、沙特阿拉伯语-英语、波斯语-英语、德语-英语），采用两阶段管道（启发式过滤+LLM评分）构建数据集，并比较了五家商业ASR提供商。ElevenLabs Scribe v2在所有语言对上取得最低WER（13.2%）和最高BERTScore（0.936），同时论证了BERTScore对阿拉伯语/波斯语对更可靠，以及难度分层分析能揭示被聚合均值掩盖的性能差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有商业ASR基准主要评估干净的单语言音频，报告单一的WER指标无法反映真实的多语言性能，而代码-switching场景是ASR最具挑战性且研究不足的方向之一，因此亟需建立专门针对代码-switching的基准测试。

Method: 采用两阶段管道构建数据集：(1) 启发式过滤器基于五个结构性代码-switching信号对转录本评分；(2) GPT-4o和Gemini 1.5 Pro集成模型从六个语言维度评分。该管道相比穷举评分节省约91%的LLM评分成本。每个数据集包含300个样本，覆盖四种语言对。使用WER和BERTScore两种指标评估五家商业ASR提供商。

Result: ElevenLabs Scribe v2在所有四个语言对上WER最低（总体13.2%，埃及阿拉伯语13.1%），BERTScore最高（总体0.936）。BERTScore对阿拉伯语和波斯语对更可靠，因为转写变异性会导致WER惩罚语义正确的转录。难度分层分析揭示了聚合均值掩盖的性能差距。BERT嵌入投影证实了参考文献与假设之间的语义接近性。

Conclusion: 代码-switching场景下不同ASR系统存在显著的性能差异，ElevenLabs Scribe v2表现出最佳性能；建议在阿拉伯语和波斯语等转写变异较大的语言对中使用BERTScore替代WER；采用难度分层分析能更全面地评估ASR系统的实际表现。

Abstract: Code-switching -- the natural alternation between two languages within a single utterance -- represents one of the most challenging and under-studied conditions for automatic speech recognition (ASR). Existing commercial ASR benchmarks predominantly evaluate clean, monolingual audio and report a single Word Error Rate (WER) figure that tells practitioners little about real-world multilingual performance. We present a benchmark evaluating five commercial ASR providers across four language pairs: Egyptian Arabic--English, Saudi Arabic (Najdi/Hijazi)--English, Persian (Farsi)--English, and German--English. Each dataset comprises 300 samples selected by a two-stage pipeline: a heuristic filter scoring transcripts on five structural code-switching signals, followed by a GPT-4o and Gemini 1.5 Pro ensemble scoring candidates across six linguistic dimensions. This pipeline reduces LLM scoring costs by approximately 91\% relative to exhaustive scoring. We evaluate the systems on both WER and BERTScore, arguing that BERTScore is a more reliable metric for Arabic and Persian pairs where transliteration variance causes WER to penalise semantically correct transcriptions. ElevenLabs Scribe v2 achieves the lowest WER across all four language pairs (13.2% overall; 13.1% on Egyptian Arabic) and leads on BERTScore (0.936 overall). We further demonstrate that difficulty-stratified analysis reveals performance gaps masked by aggregate averages, and that BERT embedding projections confirm semantic proximity between reference and hypothesis despite surface-level script differences. The benchmarking dataset is publicly available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/Perle-ai/ASR_Code_Switch.

</details>


### [92] [ReacTOD: Bounded Neuro-Symbolic Agentic NLU for Zero-Shot Dialogue State Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19077)
*Yanjun Lin,Zimo Xiao,Kartik Natarajan,Mahesh Sankaranarayanan,Niraj Nawanit,Rakshit Parashar,Austin Zhang,Karthik Konaraddi,Rishita Mote,Wei Niu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ReacTOD提出一种有界神经符号架构，将NLU重构为离散工具调用，结合自纠正ReAct循环与确定性验证，在MultiWOZ和SGD基准上实现零样本SOTA，显著提升任务型对话系统的准确性和可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中等规模LLM在实际延迟约束下易产生幻觉和格式错误，导致对话系统执行错误操作（如酒店预订日期错误），需要一种可预测、可纠错且轻量的架构。

Method: 1) 将NLU重构为ReAct循环中的离散工具调用，实现迭代自纠正；2) 符号验证器在每次对话状态更新时强制检查动作合规性、模式一致性和共指一致性；3) 增量状态预测与按需历史检索压缩提示长度，提升指令遵循能力。

Result: MultiWOZ 2.1上零样本SOTA：gpt-oss-20B达52.71% JGA（超此前最优14百分点），Qwen3-8B达47.34%；SGD上Claude-Opus-4.6达80.68% JGA，Qwen3-32B达64.09%。错误自纠正率93.1%，单次推理准确率提升最多9.3百分点。

Conclusion: ReacTOD通过神经符号混合架构有效结合了LLM的灵活性与符号系统的可靠性，在参数受限模型上仍能取得显著性能提升，展示了跨基准泛化能力，无需任务特定训练数据。

Abstract: Task-oriented dialogue systems -- handling transactions, reservations, and service requests -- require predictable behavior, yet the moderately-sized LLMs needed for practical latency are prone to hallucination and format errors that cascade into incorrect actions (e.g., a hotel booked for the wrong date). We propose ReacTOD, a bounded neuro-symbolic architecture that reformulates NLU as discrete tool calls within a self-correcting ReAct loop governed by deterministic validation. A bounded ReAct loop enables iterative self-correction, improving accuracy by up to 9.3 percentage points over single-pass inference on MultiWOZ. A symbolic validator enforces action compliance, schema conformance, and coreference consistency on every dialogue state update, achieving a 93.1% self-correction rate on intercepted errors and producing structured execution traces. Incremental state prediction and on-demand history retrieval keep prompts compact, empirically improving instruction adherence in parameter-constrained models. On MultiWOZ 2.1, ReacTOD achieves a new zero-shot state-of-the-art: gpt-oss-20B reaches 52.71% joint goal accuracy, surpassing the previous best by 14 percentage points, while Qwen3-8B achieves 47.34% with only 8B parameters. On the Schema-Guided Dialogue (SGD) benchmark, ReacTOD with Claude-Opus-4.6 achieves 80.68% JGA under fully end-to-end evaluation with predicted domains, and Qwen3-32B reaches 64.09% -- demonstrating cross-benchmark generalization without task-specific training data.

</details>


### [93] [Agent Meltdowns: The Road to Hell Is Paved with Helpful Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19149)
*Rishi Jha,Harold Triedman,Arkaprabha Bhattacharya,Vitaly Shmatikov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发现并系统测量了LLM智能体在遭遇良性环境错误（如网页无法访问、文件缺失等）时会产生一种新型故障——"意外熔毁"（accidental meltdown），即非对抗性输入下出现不安全或有害行为。通过对GPT、Grok、Gemini等智能体系统的评估，发现64.7%的智能体在遭遇模拟错误时出现熔毁，其中超过一半的不安全行为未向用户报告，且错误引发的探索行为与不安全行为存在相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可靠性和安全基准未能捕获智能体在遭遇良性环境错误时产生的非对抗性不安全行为，需要系统性地定义、分类和度量这类新型故障模式。

Method: 构建了熔毁行为分类体系，开发了智能体无关的模拟错误注入基础设施，在GPT、Grok、Gemini等系统上系统注入本地和远程模拟错误并进行评估。

Result: 64.7%的智能体遭遇模拟错误时发生熔毁（如未授权侦察、绕过访问控制等），超过半数不安全行为未被报告给用户，且探索行为与不安全行为正相关。

Conclusion: 意外熔毁是现有基准未覆盖的普遍且严重的智能体安全问题，错误引发的探索行为是导致不安全行为的关键因素，需要新的安全评估和防护机制。

Abstract: Agents operating with computer and Web use inevitably encounter errors: inaccessible webpages, missing files, local and remote misconfigurations, etc. These errors do not thwart agents based on state-of-the-art models. They helpfully continue to look for ways to complete their tasks.
  We introduce, characterize, and measure a new type of agent failure we call \emph{accidental meltdown}: unsafe or harmful behavior in response to a benign environmental error, in the absence of any adversarial inputs. Because meltdowns are not captured by the existing reliability or safety benchmarks, we develop a taxonomy of meltdown behaviors. We then implement an agent-agnostic infrastructure for injecting simulated local and remote errors into the rollout environment and use it to systematically evaluate agent systems powered by GPT, Grok, and Gemini.
  Our evaluation demonstrates that meltdowns (e.g., conducting unauthorized reconnaissance or subverting access control) of varying severity and success occur in 64.7\% of agent rollouts that encounter simulated errors, spanning all combinations of agent system, backing model, and error type. In over half of these meltdowns, unsafe behaviors are not reported to the user. Comparing behaviors of the same agents with and without errors, we find that exploration in response to errors is correlated with unsafe and harmful behavior.

</details>


### [94] [Prompting language influences diagnostic reasoning and accuracy of large language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19173)
*Adrien Bazoge,Josselin Corvellec,Sofiane Djillali Sid-Ahmed,Pierre-Antoine Gourraud*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究评估了五种大语言模型（o3、DeepSeek-R1、GPT-4-Turbo、Llama-3.1-405B-Instruct、BioMistral-7B）在英语和法语提示下对180个临床病例的诊断推理和最终诊断准确性。除o3外，其余四种模型在英语下表现显著优于法语，提示语言是影响LLM临床性能的关键因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在临床决策支持中的应用日益增多，但大多数评估仅在英语环境下进行，其在其他语言中的可靠性尚不明确，这限制了其在全球范围内的公平部署。

Method: 采用180个涵盖16个医学专业的临床病例，由两名医生使用18分量表评估五种LLM（o3、DeepSeek-R1、GPT-4-Turbo、Llama-3.1-405B-Instruct、BioMistral-7B）在英语和法语提示下的诊断准确性和推理质量。

Result: 四种模型在英语下表现优于法语（平均差异0.37-0.91，校正后p<0.05），差距体现在鉴别诊断、逻辑结构和内部有效性等多个推理维度。仅o3模型未表现出显著的语言效应。

Conclusion: 提示语言是LLM临床性能的关键决定因素，这对全球语言文化公平部署具有重要意义。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly explored for clinical decision support, yet most evaluations are conducted in English, leaving their reliability in other languages uncertain. Here we evaluate the impact of prompting language on diagnostic reasoning and final diagnosis accuracy by comparing English and French performance across five LLMs (o3, DeepSeek-R1, GPT-4-Turbo, Llama-3.1-405B-Instruct, and BioMistral-7B). A total of 180 clinical vignettes covering 16 medical specialties were assessed by two physicians using an 18-point scale evaluating both diagnosis accuracy and reasoning quality. Four of the five models performed better in English (mean difference 0.37-0.91, adjusted p < 0.05), with the gap spanning multiple aspects of reasoning, including differential diagnosis, logical structure, and internal validity. o3 was the only model showing no overall language effect. These findings demonstrate that prompting language remains a critical determinant of LLM clinical performance, with implications for equitable linguistico-cultural deployment worldwide.

</details>


### [95] [MMoA: An AI-Agent framework with recurrence for Memoried Mixure-of-Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19194)
*Rui Chu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了MMoA，一种基于LSTM的门控循环路由架构，用于多智能体LLM系统中的自适应智能体选择，在保持接近传统MoA精度的同时提升了计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MoA系统使用静态路由器，无法充分捕获跨聚合层的时间依赖和上下文依赖关系，限制了智能体聚合的灵活性。

Method: 将LSTM式门控机制集成到路由器中，构建循环路由架构，使智能体的贡献权重既依赖于当前输入，也依赖于历史路由决策。

Result: 在AlpacaEval 2.0上MMoA胜率58.0%（MoA为59.8%），计算开销降低多达4.6%。

Conclusion: MMoA提供了一种可扩展且高效的方案，用于构建自适应多智能体LLM系统。

Abstract: The Mixture-of-Agents (MoA) framework has shown promise in improving large language model (LLM) performance by aggregating outputs from multiple agents. However, existing MoA systems often rely on static routers that do not fully capture temporal and contextual dependencies across aggregation layers. To address this limitation, we propose MMoA, a recurrent MoA architecture that integrates LSTM-based gating into the agent selection process. The recurrence router adaptively modulates agent contributions based on both current inputs and historical routing decisions, enabling more context-aware aggregation. We evaluate MMoA on standard instruction-following benchmarks, including AlpacaEval 2.0, MT-Bench, and Arena-Hard. The results show that MMoA achieves comparable accuracy to traditional MoA while reducing computational overhead by dynamically activating fewer agents. For example, on AlpacaEval 2.0, MMoA achieves a win rate of 58.0%, compared with 59.8% for MoA, while improving runtime efficiency by up to 4.6%. These results suggest that MMoA provides a scalable and efficient approach for adaptive multi-agent LLM systems.

</details>


### [96] [Time to REFLECT: Can We Trust LLM Judges for Evidence-based Research Agents?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19196)
*Leyao Wang,Yanan He,Peng Chen,Asaf Yehudai,Yixin Liu,Rex Ying,Michal Shmueli-Scheuer,Arman Cohan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出REFLECT基准，通过细粒度故障注入评估LLM裁判在深度研究智能体评估中的可靠性，发现当前最佳模型整体准确率低于55%，尤其在证据验证方面表现不佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM作为裁判在评估深度研究智能体时可靠性未知，现有元评估方法存在两个不足：(1)依赖粗粒度、主观的人类偏好一致性；(2)聚焦指令遵循或可验证任务，忽略了开放式的智能体执行场景。

Method: 提出REFLECT基准，定义细粒度的过程和结果级故障模式分类体系，通过对经过质量筛选的智能体执行轨迹进行可控、局部干预，生成可验证、全面、细粒度的评估实例。

Result: 当前最佳LLM裁判在推理、工具使用和报告质量故障上的整体准确率低于55%，在证据验证方面表现尤其差。

Conclusion: 揭示了LLM裁判的系统性局限，展示了成本与可靠性之间的权衡，为构建更可靠的深度研究智能体评估流水线提供了可操作的指导。

Abstract: Deep research agents increasingly automate complex information-seeking tasks, producing evidence-grounded reports via multi-step reasoning, tool use, and synthesis. Their growing role demands scalable, reliable evaluation, positioning LLM-as-judge as a supervision paradigm for assessing factual accuracy, evidence use, and reasoning quality. Yet the reliability of these judges for deep research agents remains poorly understood, posing a critical meta-evaluation problem: before deploying LLM judges to supervise research agents, we must first evaluate the judges themselves. Existing meta-evaluations fall short in two ways: (1) reliance on coarse, subjective human-preference agreement; (2) focus on instruction-following or verifiable tasks, leaving open-ended agent executions unexplored. To address these gaps, we introduce REFLECT (REliable Fine-grained LLM judge Evaluation via Controlled inTervention), a meta-evaluation benchmark targeting fine-grained failure detection in agentic environments. REFLECT defines a detailed taxonomy of process- and outcome-level failure modes, instantiated by performing controlled and localized interventions on quality-screened agent execution traces. This yields verifiable, comprehensive, and fine-grained instances for validating the judge models. Our experiments show that current LLM judges remain unreliable: even the best-performing models achieve overall accuracies below 55% across reasoning, tool-use, and report-quality failures, with especially poor performance on evidence verification. Together, our taxonomy and findings expose systematic judge limitations, reveal tradeoffs in cost and reliability, and offer actionable guidance for building more reliable evaluation pipelines for deep research agents.

</details>


### [97] [Fine-tuning language encoding models on slow fMRI improves prediction for fast ECoG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19224)
*Aditya R. Vaidya,Richard J. Antonello,Alexander G. Huth*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出利用非侵入式fMRI数据来弥合侵入式ECoG数据的训练差距。通过在fMRI上微调语言表示模型，构建ECoG编码模型，发现即使fMRI时间分辨率比ECoG低两个数量级，仍能显著提升ECoG预测性能，且性能随fMRI数据量增加而持续提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ECoG等颅内脑电记录方法具有高时空分辨率，但受限于需要植入电极的特定患者群体，导致训练数据获取困难且样本量有限。作者希望通过结合易于获取的非侵入式fMRI数据来缓解这一数据瓶颈。

Method: 1) 使用在fMRI数据上微调的语言表示模型来构建ECoG编码模型；2) 将fMRI数据时间下采样2倍后微调模型，测试泛化能力；3) 评估fMRI微调数据量对ECoG预测性能的scaling效应。

Result: 1) fMRI微调的表示显著提升了ECoG预测性能，且超出fMRI直接测量的频带范围；2) 时间下采样2倍的fMRI模型仍能达到与原始模型相当的预测水平；3) ECoG预测性能随fMRI数据量增加而持续提升。

Conclusion: "慢"数据（fMRI）可以作为构建"快"数据（ECoG）模型的宝贵资源，跨模态整合多种记录方法有望进一步提升解码等应用的表现。

Abstract: Neuroscientists have recently turned to intracranial brain recording methods, like electrocorticography (ECoG), for human experiments because of the fine spatial and temporal resolution that they afford. Models trained on this data, however, are fundamentally restricted by the patient populations that can receive the implants necessary for recording. We propose using non-invasive fMRI to bridge the gap in training data. Using spoken language representations fine-tuned on fMRI, we build encoding models of ECoG. These representations showed improved prediction performance in ECoG, even though the temporal resolution of fMRI is two orders of magnitude worse. Prediction improved in frequency bands well beyond what is directly measured in fMRI. Next, to test the procedure's generalization ability, we fine-tuned models on fMRI responses that were temporally downsampled by a factor of 2. Despite the loss in resolution, these models were able to predict fMRI and ECoG responses at levels comparable to the original fMRI-tuned models. Finally, we showed that ECoG performance steadily scales with the amount of fMRI-tuning data. Our results show that "slow" data like fMRI can be a valuable resource for building better models of "fast" brain data like ECoG. In the future, integrating across multiple recording methods may further improve performance in other applications, like decoding.

</details>


### [98] [Diagnosing Multi-step Reasoning Failures in Black-box LLMs via Stepwise Confidence Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19228)
*Xiaoou Liu,Tiejin Chen,Dengjia Zhang,Yaqing Wang,Lu Cheng,Hua Wei*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出SCA框架，通过信息瓶颈原理为闭源LLM的推理步骤分配置信度，无需内部模型访问，能有效识别低置信步骤并提升自纠正成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM在推理任务中表现优异，但多步推理过程的错误诊断困难；现有置信度估计方法仅关注最终答案或需要模型内部访问，无法在闭源模型上对推理步骤进行细粒度评估。

Method: 提出Stepwise Confidence Attribution (SCA)框架，基于信息瓶颈(IB)原理：与正确解共识结构对齐的步骤获高置信度，偏离步骤被标记为潜在错误。包含两种互补方法：(1) NIBS——非参数IB方法，无需图结构度量一致性；(2) GIBS——基于图的IB模型，通过可微分掩码学习子图以捕获逻辑变异性。

Result: 在数学推理和多跳问答任务上，SCA能可靠识别与推理错误高度相关的低置信步骤；利用步骤级置信度引导自纠正，成功率和纠正成功率相比答案级反馈提升高达13.5%。

Conclusion: SCA框架为闭源LLM提供了有效的步骤级置信度估计方法，不仅可诊断推理错误，还能显著提升模型自纠正能力，弥补了现有方法在细粒度推理诊断上的空白。

Abstract: Large Language Models have achieved strong performance on reasoning tasks with objective answers by generating step-by-step solutions, but diagnosing where a multi-step reasoning trace might fail remains difficult. Confidence estimation offers a diagnostic signal, yet existing methods are restricted to final answers or require internal model access. In this paper, we introduce Stepwise Confidence Attribution (SCA), a framework for closed-source LLMs that assigns step-level confidence based only on generated reasoning traces. SCA applies the Information Bottleneck principle: steps aligning with consensus structures across correct solutions receive high confidence, while deviations are flagged as potentially erroneous. We propose two complementary methods: (1) NIBS, a non-parametric IB approach measuring consistency without graph structures, and (2) GIBS, a graph-based IB model that learns subgraphs through a differentiable mask to capture logical variability. Extensive experiments on mathematical reasoning and multi-hop question answering show that SCA reliably identifies low-confidence steps strongly correlated with reasoning errors. Moreover, using step-level confidence to guide self-correction improves the correction success rate by up to 13.5\% over answer-level feedback.

</details>


### [99] [FormalASR: End-to-End Spoken Chinese to Formal Text](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19266)
*Wanyi Ning,Yinshang Guo,Haitao Qian,Jiyuan Cheng,Weiyuan Feng,Yufei Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出FormalASR，两个紧凑的端到端模型（0.6B和1.7B），能够将中文口语直接转写为正式书面文本，无需后处理LLM，在部署时更轻量、适合设备端使用。通过构建两个大规模口语转正式数据集（WenetSpeech-Formal和Speechio-Formal），并微调Qwen3-ASR，实验显示相比逐字转录基线，CER相对降低37.4%，ROUGE-L和BERTScore也有所提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统ASR系统针对逐字转录优化，保留了口语中的不流畅、填充词和非正式结构，不适合下游写作类应用。现有两阶段ASR+LLM后编辑方案延迟高、内存成本大，难以部署在设备端。因此需要一种端到端的轻量级解决方案，直接将口语转录为正式书面文本。

Method: 1) 构建两个大规模口语转正式数据集WenetSpeech-Formal和Speechio-Formal，采用基于LLM的重写和质量过滤；2) 基于Qwen3-ASR在0.6B和1.7B两个规模上进行监督微调，得到两个紧凑的端到端模型FormalASR。

Result: 在WenetSpeech-Formal和Speechio-Formal数据集上，FormalASR相比逐字转录基线实现最高37.4%的CER相对降低，同时ROUGE-L和BERTScore也有提升。

Conclusion: FormalASR无需后处理LLM，提供了一种轻量级、适合设备端部署的口语转正式文本解决方案，有效解决了传统两阶段流水线的延迟和内存开销问题。

Abstract: Automatic speech recognition (ASR) systems are typically optimized for verbatim transcription, which preserves disfluencies, filler words, and informal spoken structures that are often unsuitable for downstream writing-oriented applications. A common workaround is a two-stage ASR+LLM pipeline for post-editing, but this design increases latency and memory cost and is difficult to deploy on-device. We present FormalASR, two compact end-to-end models (0.6B and 1.7B) that directly transcribe spoken Chinese into formal written text. To enable this setting, we build WenetSpeech-Formal and Speechio-Formal, two large-scale spoken-to-formal datasets constructed by LLM-based rewriting and quality filtering. We then fine-tune Qwen3-ASR at two scales (0.6B and 1.7B) with supervised fine-tuning. Experiments on WenetSpeech-Formal and Speechio-Formal show that FormalASR achieves up to 37.4% relative CER reduction over verbatim baselines, while also improving ROUGE-L and BERTScore. FormalASR requires no post-processing LLM at deployment time, providing a lightweight, on-device solution for spoken-to-formal transcription.

</details>


### [100] [DECOR: Auditing LLM Deception via Information Manipulation Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19270)
*Linyue Cai,Samuel Yeh,Jwala Dhamala,Rahul Gupta,Sharon Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出DECOR，一个基于信息操纵理论的多智能体框架，用于细粒度审计LLM回应中的策略性欺骗。DECOR将输入上下文分解为原子信息单元，并在四个操纵维度上对每个单元评分，生成可解释的操纵画像并聚合为全局欺骗指数。在单轮和多轮欺骗检测基准上均达到SOTA，泛化至15个前沿模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有黑盒欺骗检测方法依赖粗粒度判断，可解释性有限，无法准确定位哪些事实被扭曲以及如何被扭曲。需要一种细粒度、可解释且理论驱动的审计方法。

Method: 基于信息操纵理论(IMT)构建多智能体框架DECOR：(1) 将输入上下文分解为原子信息单元；(2) 在每个单元上从四个操纵维度(隐瞒、歪曲、转移焦点、模糊化)进行评分；(3) 生成可解释的操纵画像；(4) 聚合为全局欺骗指数。

Result: 在单轮和多轮欺骗检测基准上均达到SOTA性能，超越强基线方法；泛化至15个前沿LLM；消融实验验证了各关键设计组件的贡献。

Conclusion: 细粒度、理论驱动的信息操纵审计为LLM欺骗检测提供了有效且可解释的路径。

Abstract: Large language models can deceive by subtly manipulating truthful information -- omitting key facts, shifting focus, or obscuring meaning -- making such behavior difficult to detect. Existing black-box methods rely on coarse-grained judgments, offering limited interpretability and failing to pinpoint which facts were distorted and how. We introduce DECOR, a multi-agent framework grounded in Information Manipulation Theory for fine-grained auditing of strategic deception in LLM responses. DECOR decomposes input contexts into atomic informational units and scores each unit against the response across four dimensions of manipulation, producing interpretable manipulation profiles that are aggregated into a global deception index. We comprehensively evaluate DECOR on both single-turn and multi-turn deception detection benchmarks spanning real-world domains, and show that DECOR achieves state-of-the-art performance on both, outperforming competitive baselines. The framework generalizes across 15 frontier models, and ablation studies confirm the contribution of each key design component. Our findings demonstrate that fine-grained, theory-grounded auditing of information manipulation offers an effective and interpretable path for LLM deception detection.

</details>


### [101] [Lost in Interpretation: The Plausibility-Faithfulness Trade-off in Cross-Lingual Explanations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19274)
*Somnath Banerjee,Pranav Jha,Rima Hazra,Animesh Mukherjee*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文研究了多语言LLM在非英语输入下使用英语解释进行审计的现象，发现英语解释与人类标注的跨度一致性更高，但其因果归因更弱（全面性下降最多5.7倍），且在社会敏感分类任务中丢失语用线索。建议在输入语言中审计解释，并报告多维度忠实度指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多语言LLM部署广泛，但审计时常用英语解释替代非英语输入的真实解释，这种做法尚未被系统性评估其忠实性和可靠性。

Method: 在3个任务、5种语言、2个多语言LLM家族上，对比英语解释与本地语言解释在跨度一致性、全面性、充分性等指标上的差异。

Result: 英语解释的跨度一致性更高，但全面性最多下降5.7倍；社会分类任务中，英语解释丢失语用线索，忠实度和跨度一致性均下降。

Conclusion: 应使用输入语言进行解释审计，报告多维度忠实度指标，并将英语解释视为沟通总结而非忠实决策轨迹。

Abstract: LLMs deployed multilingually are often audited via English explanations for non-English inputs. We evaluate extractive explanations ''where the model identifies input token spans as evidence alongside a generated rationale'' and uncover a systematic trade-off: English-pivot explanations can achieve higher span agreement with human rationales while their evidence becomes less causally grounded in the model's prediction, as measured by both comprehensiveness and sufficiency. Across 3 tasks, 5~languages, and 2~multilingual LLM families, we find that English explanations frequently produce fluent but loosely anchored rationales, with comprehensiveness degrading by up to 5.7x relative to native-language conditions - even as task accuracy remains stable across settings. For socially nuanced classification, English pivots also fail to preserve pragmatic cues, reducing both faithfulness and span agreement. We recommend auditing explanations in the input language, reporting multi-faceted faithfulness metrics beyond lexical overlap, and treating English rationales as communication summaries rather than faithful decision traces.

</details>


### [102] [OpenCompass: A Universal Evaluation Platform for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19276)
*Maosong Cao,Kai Chen,Haodong Duan,Yixiao Fang,Tong Gao,Ge Jiaye,Mo Li,Hongwei Liu,Junnan Liu,Yuan Liu,Chengqi Lyu,Han Lyu,Ningsheng Ma,Zerun Ma,Yu Sun,Zhiyong Wu,Linchen Xiao,Jun Xu,Haochen Ye,Zhaohui Yu,Yike Yuan,Songyang Zhang,Yufeng Zhao,Fengzhe Zhou,Peiheng Zhou,Dongsheng Zhu,Lin Zhu,Jingming Zhuo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了OpenCompass，一个一站式、可扩展、高并发的通用大语言模型评估平台，通过模块化设计和组件解耦，解决当前静态基准评估方法中任务类型多样、标准不一致、流程碎片化等问题，支持多领域主流基准数据集，为学术界和工业界提供统一的LLM评估工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前主流静态基准数据集评估方法面临任务类型多样、评估标准不一致、数据和处理流程碎片化等挑战，难以高效进行跨域和大规模模型评估，亟需一个统一、高效的评估平台。

Method: OpenCompass采用模块化和组件解耦的设计理念，核心架构包括配置系统、任务划分模块、执行与调度模块、任务执行单元和结果可视化模块；工作流提供基于规则、LLM-as-a-Judge和级联评估器，适应不同任务场景。

Result: 平台具备高兼容性、灵活性和高并发三大核心优势，支持知识、推理、计算、科学、语言、代码等多领域主流基准数据集，实现统一高效的LLM评估。

Conclusion: OpenCompass为学术界和工业界提供统一的LLM评估工具，有助于准确识别LLM的优缺点并促进后续优化。

Abstract: In recent years, the field of artificial intelligence has undergone a paradigm shift from task-specific small-scale models to general-purpose large language models (LLMs). With the rapid iteration of LLMs, objective, quantitative, and comprehensive evaluation of their capabilities has become a critical link in advancing technological development. Currently, the mainstream static benchmark dataset-based evaluation methods face challenges such as the diversity of task types, inconsistent evaluation criteria, and fragmentation of data and processing workflows, making it difficult to efficiently conduct cross-domain and large-scale model evaluation. To address the aforementioned issues, this paper proposes and open-sources OpenCompass, a one-stop, scalable, and high-concurrency-supported general-purpose LLM evaluation platform. Adhering to the design philosophy of modularization and component decoupling, the platform boasts three core advantages: high compatibility, flexibility, and high concurrency. The core architecture of OpenCompass comprises five key components: the Configuration System, Task Partitioning Module, Execution and Scheduling Module, Task Execution Unit, and Result Visualization Module. Its workflow provides rule-based, LLM-as-a-Judge, and cascaded evaluators to adapt to the requirements of different task scenarios. Supporting mainstream benchmark datasets across multiple domains, including knowledge, reasoning, computation, science, language, code, etc., the platform offers a unified and efficient LLM evaluation tool for both academia and industry, facilitating the accurate identification of strengths and weaknesses of LLMs as well as their subsequent optimization.

</details>


### [103] [Language models struggle with compartmentalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19284)
*Thomas Vincent Howe,David Wingate*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LLM在训练数据中面对同一概念的不同呈现方式（如多语言、多编程语言、形式与自然语言）时，会出现区室化现象——无法识别并共享这些不同呈现之间的统计强度，导致并行学习冗余的内部表示，降低样本效率，且合成并行数据难以改善这一问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究LLM在处理同一潜在概念的不同呈现方式时，是否能够有效识别并共享统计强度，以及区室化现象对模型容量和样本效率的影响。

Method: 通过分析LLM在不同呈现方式（多语言、多编程语言、形式与自然语言）下的学习行为，定义并测量区室化程度；使用合成并行数据测试其对区室化的缓解效果；研究模型规模、呈现数量等因素对区室化的影响。

Result: LLM表现出明显的区室化现象，同一概念的不同呈现方式被学习为平行的内部表示，冗余占用模型容量并降低样本效率；合成并行数据虽易被学习但无法改善区室化；小模型中早期多语言学习几乎完全区室化；所有干预措施的效果均存在相变现象，取决于呈现方式的数量。

Conclusion: 语言建模目标可能无法一致地统一不同呈现方式下的概念表示，区室化是LLM学习中的一个固有问题，其严重程度随呈现方式数量增加而加剧，且现有干预手段效果不稳定。

Abstract: In the training data used by large language models (LLMs), the same latent concept is often presented in multiple distinct ways: the same facts appear in English and Swahili; many functions can be expressed in both Python and Haskell; we can express propositions in both formal and natural language. We show that LLMs can exhibit compartmentalization, where they fail to identify and share statistical strength between distinct presentations of unified concepts. In the worst case, LLMs simply learn parallel internal representations of each presentation of the concept, saturating model capacity with redundancies and decreasing sample efficiency with the number of such presentations. We also demonstrate that synthetic parallel data can fail to improve this despite being easily learned itself. Under this framework, we find that, for small models, early multilingual learning is nearly entirely compartmentalized. Finally, all interventions that we study exhibit a phase transition in which their effectiveness depends on the number of distinct presentations, suggesting that the language modeling objective may only inconsistently unify representations.

</details>


### [104] [Are Rationales Necessary and Sufficient? Tuning LLMs for Explainable Misinformation Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19285)
*Bing Wang,Rui Miao,Ximing Li,Chen Shen,Shaotian Yan,Changchun Li,Kaiyuan Liu,Xiaosong Yuan,Jieping Ye*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出LONSREX数据合成流水线，通过量化每个验证步骤对最终预测的贡献度，筛选出必要且充分的推理依据，用于微调专用大语言模型以实现可解释的 misinformation 检测，解决了传统基于标签正确性过滤方法中存在的推理依据不足和过度验证问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交媒体上错误信息的快速传播构成严峻挑战。现有可解释错误信息检测方法主要依赖为现成LLM设计复杂提示词来生成推理依据，但缺乏针对该任务的专用微调模型。此外，简单的基于标签正确性的数据过滤方法存在两个关键缺陷：粗粒度标签导致推理依据不足，以及强LLM的过度验证行为产生不必要的冗长推理依据。

Method: 提出LONSREX数据合成流水线：首先收集大规模事实核查文章，使用多个强LLM生成真实性预测和推理依据；然后提出一种量化每个验证步骤对最终预测贡献度的评估指标，据此筛选必要且充分的推理依据；最后用高质量数据微调专用LLM。

Result: 实验结果表明LONSREX在可解释错误信息检测任务上有效，相比传统基于标签正确性的过滤方法，能够更好地平衡推理依据的必要性与充分性，提升检测性能和解释质量。

Conclusion: 面向可解释错误信息检测，本文揭示了简单标签过滤方法的局限性，并提出了LONSREX流水线来系统性地构建高质量训练数据，为后续研究提供了新的思路和基准方法。

Abstract: The rapid spread of misinformation on social media platforms has become a formidable challenge. To mitigate its proliferation, Misinformation Detection (MD) has emerged as a critical research topic. Traditional MD approaches based on small models typically perform binary classification through a black-box process. Recently, the rise of Large Language Models (LLMs) has enabled explainable MD, where models generate rationales that explain their decisions, thereby enhancing transparency. Existing explainable MD methods primarily focus on crafting sophisticated prompts to elicit rationales from off-the-shelf LLMs. In this work, we propose a pipeline to fine-tune a dedicated LLM specifically for explainable MD. Our pipeline begins by collecting large-scale fact-checked articles, and then uses multiple strong LLMs to produce veracity predictions and rationales. To ensure high-quality training data, we leverage a filtering strategy that selects only the correct instances for fine-tuning. While this pipeline is intuitive and prevalent, our experiments reveal that naive filtering based solely on label correctness is insufficient in practice and suffers from two critical limitations: (1) Coarse-grained labels cause insufficient rationales: Rationales filtered solely based on binary labels are insufficient to adequately support their decisions; (2) Over-verification behavior causes unnecessary rationales: Stronger LLMs tend to exhibit over-verification behavior, producing excessively verbose and unnecessary rationales. To address these issues, we introduce LONSREX, a novel data synthesis pipeline to Locate Necessary and Sufficient Rationales for Explainable MD. Specifically, we propose a metric that quantifies the contribution of each verification step to the final prediction, thereby evaluating its necessity and sufficiency. Experimental results demonstrate the effectiveness of LONSREX.

</details>


### [105] [How Do Document Parsers Break? Auditing Structural Vulnerability in Document Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19309)
*Yue Chen,Yihao Wang,Ziyi Tang,Keze Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种轻量级的输出级审计框架，用于评估文档布局分析（DLA）管线的鲁棒性，解决了现有评估中存在的“足迹偏差”问题。该框架通过块级结构损失率（B-SLR）、粒度感知暴露描述符和路径归因等方法，揭示了扰动与布局结构的交互及故障传播机制。实验表明，B-SLR比扰动面积更能反映OCR不稳定性，且小范围结构定向扰动即可造成显著的检索/问答质量下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DLA鲁棒性评估存在“足迹偏差”（Footprint Bias），即评估结果过度依赖扰动面积而非结构影响，缺乏对布局结构敏感性的系统度量。

Method: 提出输出级审计框架，解耦了探针构建、策略驱动目标和结构感知诊断三个环节。具体包括：块级结构损失率（B-SLR）、粒度感知暴露描述符和路径归因分析，用于量化扰动对布局结构的影响及故障传播路径。

Result: 在MinerU和PP-StructureV3上的1000页实验中，扰动面积与OCR不稳定性的相关性较低（R²=0.384/0.110），而B-SLR与之高度相关（R²=0.727/0.916）。小范围结构定向扰动即可导致与大面积扰动相当的问答/检索性能下降。

Conclusion: DLA鲁棒性评估应从基于扰动面积的应力测试转向结构感知的脆弱性审计，以实现更精准的评估与改进方向。

Abstract: Document Layout Analysis (DLA) pipelines provide structured page representations for retrieval-augmented generation, long-document question answering, and other document intelligence systems, yet their robustness evaluation remains largely area-centric. We identify this Footprint Bias and propose a lightweight output-level auditing framework that decouples probe construction, policy-driven targeting, and structure-aware diagnosis. The framework combines Block-level Structural Loss Rate (B-SLR), granularity-aware exposure descriptors, and pathway attribution to analyze where perturbations interact with layout structure and how failures propagate. Across MinerU and PP-StructureV3 on 1,000 pages, affected area weakly tracks perturbation-induced OCR instability (R^2=0.384/0.110), whereas B-SLR aligns much more closely with it (R^2=0.727/0.916). Exposure descriptors further separate occlusion- and topology-dominant pathways, and small structurally targeted probes cause downstream QA/retrieval degradation comparable to larger-footprint perturbations. These results shift DLA robustness evaluation from footprint-based stress testing toward structure-aware vulnerability auditing.

</details>


### [106] [A Multi-Agent Framework for Feature-Constrained Difficulty Control in Reading Comprehension Item Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19316)
*Seonjeong Hwang,Jun Seo,Hyounghun Kim,Gary Geunbae Lee*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出MAFIG（多智能体特征约束题目生成框架），通过多个LLM智能体与特征专用评估器协作，迭代生成并修订符合指定难度约束的阅读理解题目，相比单智能体方法显著提升了约束满足率和难度控制效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的难度控制阅读理解题目生成方法多采用单智能体提示方式，难以一致地满足指定的特征约束，导致生成题目偏离目标难度水平。

Method: 提出MAFIG框架：多个LLM智能体与特征专用评估器协作，基于目标约束迭代生成和修订题目；并提出难度校准约束序列构建方法，实现题目难度单调递增。

Result: MAFIG生成题目在目标约束满足率上显著高于基线方法，并通过难度校准的约束序列实现了稳健的难度控制。

Conclusion: 多智能体协作框架能更有效地生成符合特征约束的难度控制题目，为阅读理解题目的自动化生成提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Recent studies in difficulty-controlled reading comprehension item generation have leveraged large language models (LLMs) to produce items by adjusting difficulty-related features. However, existing methods typically rely on a single-agent prompting approach, which often fails to consistently satisfy specified feature constraints, resulting in items that deviate from the target difficulty level. To address this limitation, we introduce MAFIG, a Multi-agent Framework for Feature-constrained Item Generation, where multiple LLM agents and feature-specific evaluators collaborate to generate and iteratively revise items based on intended constraints. Furthermore, to verify the efficacy of MAFIG in difficulty control, we propose a method for constructing a sequence of feature constraint sets that yield items with monotonically increasing difficulty. Experimental results demonstrate that MAFIG generates items that adhere to target constraints at a significantly higher rate than baselines, achieving robust difficulty control through the difficulty-calibrated constraint sequence.

</details>


### [107] [HalluWorld: A Controlled Benchmark for Hallucination via Reference World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19341)
*Emmy Liu,Varun Gangal,Michael Yu,Zhuofu Tao,Karan Singh,Sachin Kumar,Steven Y. Feng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了HalluWorld，一个基于显式参考世界公式化的可扩展基准，用于系统评估大语言模型在不同环境中的幻觉问题。通过在网格世界、国际象棋和终端任务中构建完全指定的参考世界，作者发现：前沿模型在感知性幻觉上近乎解决，但在多步状态追踪和因果前向模拟上仍然困难，且不同幻觉类型源自不同的失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有幻觉基准在总结、问答、检索增强生成和智能体交互等不同场景中操作化不一致，导致某个缓解方法在一种设置中有效但在跨上下文中是否有效不明确。同时，现有基准要么需要人工标注和固定参考（可能被记忆），要么依赖难以复现的观察设置。为了研究幻觉的根本原因，需要更可控、可扩展的评估方法。

Method: 基于显式参考世界公式化（模型在产生与参考世界相悖的可观察声明时即为幻觉），构建合成和半合成环境，其中参考世界完全指定、模型视角可控、幻觉标签自动生成。HalluWorld涵盖网格世界、国际象棋和现实终端任务，支持对世界复杂性、可观察性、时间变化和源冲突策略的可控变化，并将幻觉细分为细粒度错误类别。

Result: 前沿模型在直接观察信息的感知性幻觉上近乎解决，但在多步状态追踪和因果前向模拟上仍然困难，且扩展思考（extended thinking）不能普遍解决这些问题。在终端设置中，模型在何时应该放弃回答（abstain）上也存在困难。不同探针类型和领域中的失败模式不均匀，表明幻觉源于不同的失败模式而非单一能力缺陷。

Conclusion: 受控参考世界为衡量和减少现代语言模型中的幻觉提供了一条可扩展、可复现的路径。

Abstract: Hallucination remains a central failure mode of large language models, but existing benchmarks operationalize it inconsistently across summarization, question answering, retrieval-augmented generation, and agentic interaction. This fragmentation makes it unclear whether a mitigation that works in one setting reduces hallucinations across contexts. Current benchmarks either require human annotation and fixed references that may be memorized, or rely on observations in settings that are difficult to reproduce. To study root causes, we introduce HalluWorld, an extensible benchmark grounded in an explicit reference-world formulation: a model hallucinates when it produces an observable claim that is false with respect to this world. Building on this view, we construct synthetic and semi-synthetic environments in which the reference world is fully specified, the model's view is controlled, and hallucination labels are generated automatically. HalluWorld spans gridworlds, chess, and realistic terminal tasks, enabling controlled variation of world complexity, observability, temporal change, and source-conflict policy, and disentangling hallucinations into fine-grained error categories. We evaluate frontier and open-weight language models across these settings and find consistent patterns: perceptual hallucination on directly observed information is near-solved for frontier models, while multi-step state tracking and causal forward simulation remain difficult and are not generally solved by extended thinking. In the terminal setting, models also struggle with when to abstain. The uneven profile of failures across probe types and domains suggests that hallucinations arise from distinct failure modes rather than a single capability. Our results suggest that controlled reference worlds offer a scalable and reproducible path toward measuring and reducing hallucinations in modern language models.

</details>


### [108] [Retrieval-Augmented Linguistic Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19344)
*Yi-Fan Yeh,Linwei Tao,Minjing Dong,Tao Huang,Jialin Yu,Philip Torr,Chang Xu*
